► ANSWER                                                                                                                                                        

Based on the provided documentation files, here is the summary of the project:                                                                                  

Workflow Purpose                                                                                                                                                

The project implements the Interpretable Context Methodology (ICM). Its primary purpose is to provide a structured framework for workflow optimization,         
specifically focusing on:                                                                                                                                       

 • Context Distillation: Managing and refining project context to ensure it remains interpretable and usable by agents.                                         
 • Agent Orchestration: Providing workflows for local agent operation, development agent tasks, and session handoffs.                                           
 • Auditability: Using lightweight validation utilities and prompt templates to make agent work reproducible and auditable.                                     

Directory Structure                                                                                                                                             

The project is organized into functional modules:                                                                                                               

 • ICM/01_context/: The core operational state, containing the current state, agent briefs, session handoffs, and stable project context.                       
 • ICM/03_workflows/: Contains the logic for various workflows (Development Agent, Local Agent Orchestration, Context Distiller, etc.).                         
 • ICM/06_releases/: Tracks versioning and release history.                                                                                                     
 • ICM/08_import_tools/prompts/: A library of prompt templates for extraction, tasking, and reporting.                                                          
 • ICM/10_agent_runs/: Documentation for agent run conventions.                                                                                                 
 • ICM/XX_backend/: Backend validation scripts and tests.                                                                                                       
 • ICM/local_harness/: Local execution utilities (e.g., icm_call.py).                                                                                           

Operating Rules                                                                                                                                                 

 • Naming Conventions: Use "ProjectName" for public-facing contexts and "InternalCodename" for internal continuity; they are generally interchangeable.         
 • Source Integrity: Shared links are considered "source pointers," not durable archives. You must save local snapshots before extracting summaries or merging  
   context.                                                                                                                                                     
 • Legal/Authorship: Documentation should attribute authorship to [REDACTED_AUTHOR]. Do not assume open-source licenses; preserve proprietary language unless   
   otherwise specified.                                                                                                                                         
 • Sanitization: Maintain a clean environment by excluding node_modules, .git history, and personal identifiers (emails, LAN IPs, local paths).                 

Safest Next Action                                                                                                                                              

The safest next action is to review ICM/01_context/CURRENT_STATE.md and ICM/01_context/AGENT_BRIEF.md. These files define the current working state and the     
specific operating instructions for the agent, providing the necessary foundation before executing any workflows.

  └ local_harness/README.md (+56 -0)
     1 +# Local Harness
     2 +
     3 +This folder contains the manager-side helper scripts for supervised local-worker runs.
     4 +
     5 +## Scripts
     6 +
     7 +- `icm_call.py`: configurable one-shot worker caller for native `/completion` and OpenAI-compatible `/v1` endpoints.
     8 +- `run_single_worker.py`: executes one audited single-worker run folder and writes `OUTPUT.md` plus `METRICS.json`.
     9 +
    10 +## Configuration
    11 +
    12 +The defaults preserve the sanitized placeholder hosts from the handoff bundle. Override them per call with CLI flags or environment variables:
    13 +
    14 +```text
    15 +ICM_HANDOFF_BASE_URL
    16 +ICM_HANDOFF_URL
    17 +ICM_HANDOFF_MODEL
    18 +ICM_HANDOFF_API
    19 +```
    20 +
    21 +The same suffix pattern works for `DEEP`, `CODER`, and `ROUTER`.
    22 +
    23 +## Examples
    24 +
    25 +List models on a live OpenAI-compatible worker:
    26 +
    27 +```text
    28 +python3 local_harness/icm_call.py handoff \
    29 +  --base-url http://localhost:8083/v1 \
    30 +  --list-models
    31 +```
    32 +
    33 +Call a live worker and force final-answer output:
    34 +
    35 +```text
    36 +python3 local_harness/icm_call.py handoff \
    37 +  --base-url http://localhost:8083/v1 \
    38 +  --model gemma-4-12B-it-qat-UD-Q4_K_XL.gguf \
    39 +  --final-only \
    40 +  "Reply with exactly: ok"
    41 +```
    42 +
    43 +Run a supervised single-worker folder:
    44 +
    45 +```text
    46 +python3 local_harness/run_single_worker.py \
    47 +  10_agent_runs/2026-06-07_001_doc-audit \
    48 +  handoff \
    49 +  --base-url http://localhost:8083/v1 \
    50 +  --model gemma-4-12B-it-qat-UD-Q4_K_XL.gguf \
    51 +  --final-only \
    52 +  --init-stubs \
    53 +  "Summarize the repo's three biggest harness gaps in raw markdown with three bullets max."
    54 +```
    55 +
    56 +When the worker call succeeds, review `OUTPUT.md`, edit `REVIEW.md`, promote any approved content into `ACCEPTED.md`, and rerun `python3 XX_backend/vali
        date_agent_run.py <run-folder>` before downstream use.

      1 +#!/usr/bin/env python3
      2 +"""Execute a single-worker local-agent run into the audited folder shape."""
      3 +
      4 +from __future__ import annotations
      5 +
      6 +import argparse
      7 +import json
      8 +import sys
      9 +from datetime import datetime, timezone
     10 +from pathlib import Path
     11 +from typing import Sequence
     12 +
     13 +PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
     14 +sys.path.insert(0, str(PROJECT_ROOT / "XX_backend"))
     15 +
     16 +from validate_agent_run import validate_run_folder  # type: ignore  # noqa: E402
     17 +from icm_call import DEFAULT_WORKERS, call_worker, resolve_worker_spec  # noqa: E402
     18 +
     19 +
     20 +REQUIRED_INPUT_FILES = ("TASK.md", "INPUT.md", "MODEL_REQUEST.md")
     21 +REVIEW_STUB = """# Manager Review
     22 +
     23 +## Status
     24 +- pending
     25 +
     26 +## Notes
     27 +- Review not completed yet.
     28 +"""
     29 +
     30 +ACCEPTED_STUB = """# Accepted Artifact
     31 +
     32 +Manager review is still pending. Do not reuse this file as downstream context yet.
     33 +"""
     34 +
     35 +
     36 +def estimate_tokens(text: str) -> int:
     37 +    return max(1, (len(text) + 3) // 4)
     38 +
     39 +
     40 +def ensure_trailing_newline(text: str) -> str:
     41 +    return text if text.endswith("\n") else text + "\n"
     42 +
     43 +
     44 +def write_if_missing(path: Path, content: str) -> None:
     45 +    if not path.exists():
     46 +        path.write_text(ensure_trailing_newline(content), encoding="utf-8")
     47 +
     48 +
     49 +def scaffold_required_files(run_folder: Path, prompt_text: str | None) -> None:
     50 +    write_if_missing(
     51 +        run_folder / "TASK.md",
     52 +        "# Local Agent Task\n\nPopulate this audit record before promoting the run.\n",
     53 +    )
     54 +    write_if_missing(
     55 +        run_folder / "INPUT.md",
     56 +        "# Input Bundle\n\nList the files, excerpts, or repo paths given to the worker.\n",
     57 +    )
     58 +    if prompt_text is not None:
     59 +        write_if_missing(run_folder / "MODEL_REQUEST.md", prompt_text)
     60 +    else:
     61 +        write_if_missing(
     62 +            run_folder / "MODEL_REQUEST.md",
     63 +            "# Model Request\n\nWrite the compact worker prompt here.\n",
     64 +        )
     65 +
     66 +
     67 +def missing_inputs(run_folder: Path) -> list[str]:
     68 +    return [name for name in REQUIRED_INPUT_FILES if not (run_folder / name).is_file()]
     69 +
     70 +
     71 +def build_metrics(
     72 +    run_folder: Path,
     73 +    worker: str,
     74 +    prompt_text: str,
     75 +    response,
     76 +) -> dict[str, object]:
     77 +    return {
     78 +        "timestamp_utc": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
     79 +        "run_folder": str(run_folder),
     80 +        "worker": worker,
     81 +        "status": response.status,
     82 +        "request_url": response.request_url,
     83 +        "model": response.model,
     84 +        "finish_reason": response.finish_reason,
     85 +        "usage": response.usage,
     86 +        "timings": response.timings,
     87 +        "input_est_tokens": estimate_tokens(prompt_text),
     88 +        "output_est_tokens": estimate_tokens(response.content),
     89 +        "token_estimate_method": "characters_divided_by_4",
     90 +        "error": response.error,
     91 +    }
     92 +
     93 +
     94 +def build_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
     95 +    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
     96 +        description="Run a single local worker against MODEL_REQUEST.md and write audited artifacts.",
     97 +    )
     98 +    parser.add_argument("run_folder", help="Target run folder under 10_agent_runs or another path.")
     99 +    parser.add_argument(
    100 +        "worker",
    101 +        choices=sorted(DEFAULT_WORKERS),
    102 +        help="Worker key to use for the request.",
    103 +    )
    104 +    parser.add_argument("--api", help="Override the worker API style.")
    105 +    parser.add_argument("--base-url", help="Override the worker base URL, such as http://localhost:8083/v1")
    106 +    parser.add_argument("--url", help="Override the exact completion URL.")
    107 +    parser.add_argument("--model", help="Override the model name sent to the worker.")
    108 +    parser.add_argument("--prompt-file", help="Seed MODEL_REQUEST.md from this file when scaffolding.")
    109 +    parser.add_argument("--max-tokens", type=int, default=512, help="Maximum response tokens.")
    110 +    parser.add_argument("--timeout", type=int, default=900, help="HTTP timeout in seconds.")
    111 +    parser.add_argument(
    112 +        "--final-only",
    113 +        action="store_true",
    114 +        help="Append /no_think when the prompt does not already include it.",
    115 +    )
    116 +    parser.add_argument(
    117 +        "--init-stubs",
    118 +        action="store_true",
    119 +        help="Create missing TASK.md, INPUT.md, MODEL_REQUEST.md, REVIEW.md, and ACCEPTED.md files.",
    120 +    )
    121 +    parser.add_argument(
    122 +        "prompt",
    123 +        nargs="*",
    124 +        help="Prompt text used only when scaffolding a missing MODEL_REQUEST.md.",
    125 +    )
    126 +    return parser
    127 +
    128 +
    129 +def main(argv: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> int:
    130 +    parser = build_parser()
    131 +    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    132 +
    133 +    run_folder = Path(args.run_folder)
    134 +    run_folder.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    135 +
    136 +    prompt_text: str | None = None
    137 +    if args.prompt_file:
    138 +        prompt_text = Path(args.prompt_file).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    139 +    elif args.prompt:
    140 +        prompt_text = " ".join(args.prompt)
    141 +
    142 +    if args.init_stubs:
    143 +        scaffold_required_files(run_folder, prompt_text)
    144 +
    145 +    missing = missing_inputs(run_folder)
    146 +    if missing:
    147 +        print(f"Run folder is missing required input files: {', '.join(missing)}")
    148 +        return 1
    149 +
    150 +    request_path = run_folder / "MODEL_REQUEST.md"
    151 +    request_text = request_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    152 +    spec = resolve_worker_spec(
    153 +        args.worker,
    154 +        base_url=args.base_url,
    155 +        url=args.url,
    156 +        model=args.model,
    157 +        api=args.api,
    158 +        final_only=args.final_only,
    159 +    )
    160 +    response = call_worker(
    161 +        spec,
    162 +        request_text,
    163 +        args.max_tokens,
    164 +        timeout=args.timeout,
    165 +    )
    166 +
    167 +    (run_folder / "OUTPUT.md").write_text(ensure_trailing_newline(response.content), encoding="utf-8")
    168 +    (run_folder / "METRICS.json").write_text(
    169 +        json.dumps(build_metrics(run_folder, args.worker, request_text, response), indent=2) + "\n",
    170 +        encoding="utf-8",
    171 +    )
    172 +    if args.init_stubs:
    173 +        write_if_missing(run_folder / "REVIEW.md", REVIEW_STUB)
    174 +        write_if_missing(run_folder / "ACCEPTED.md", ACCEPTED_STUB)
    175 +
    176 +    validation = validate_run_folder(run_folder)
    177 +    if validation.valid:
    178 +        print(f"Validated run folder: {run_folder}")
    179 +    else:
    180 +        print(f"Invalid run folder: {run_folder}")
    181 +        if validation.path_error:
    182 +            print(f"Error: {validation.path_error}")
    183 +        if validation.missing_files:
    184 +            print("Missing required files:")
    185 +            for filename in validation.missing_files:
    186 +                print(f"- {filename}")
    187 +
    188 +    return 0 if response.status == "ok" and validation.valid else 1
    189 +
    190 +
    191 +if __name__ == "__main__":
    192 +    raise SystemExit(main())

     38 +def ensure_trailing_newline(text: str) -> str:
     39 +    return text if text.endswith("\n") else text + "\n"
     40 +
     41 +
     42 +def write_if_missing(path: Path, content: str) -> None:

     92 +    for read_path in args.read:
     93 +        command.extend(["--read", read_path])

     98 +def build_metrics(
     99 +    run_folder: Path,
    100 +    command: Sequence[str],
    101 +    args: argparse.Namespace,

    116 +        "error": result.stderr or None,
    117 +    }
    118 +
    119 +
    120 +def build_output_text(result: subprocess.CompletedProcess[str]) -> str:
    121 +    sections: list[str] = []
    122 +    if result.stdout:
    123 +        sections.append(result.stdout.rstrip())
    124 +    if result.stderr:
    125 +        sections.append("STDERR:\n" + result.stderr.rstrip())
    126 +    if not sections:
    127 +        sections.append("[no output]")
    128 +    return "\n\n".join(sections) + "\n"
    129 +
    130 +
    131 +def build_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    132 +    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
    133 +        description="Run a supervised Aider task from MODEL_REQUEST.md into an audited run folder.",
    134 +    )

    136 +    parser.add_argument("files", nargs="*", help="Files Aider may edit.")

    163 +    )

    169 +    )
    170 +    parser.add_argument(

    181 +
    182 +
    183 +def parse_args(parser: argparse.ArgumentParser, argv: Sequence[str] | None) -> argparse.Namespace:
    184 +    if hasattr(parser, "parse_intermixed_args"):
    185 +        return parser.parse_intermixed_args(argv)

    187 +
    188 +
    189 +def main(argv: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> int:
    190 +    parser = build_parser()
    191 +    args = parse_args(parser, argv)
    192 +    args.run_folder.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    193 +
    194 +    if args.init_stubs:
    195 +        scaffold_required_files(args.run_folder)
    196 +
    197 +    write_if_missing(args.run_folder / "REVIEW.md", REVIEW_STUB)
    198 +    write_if_missing(args.run_folder / "ACCEPTED.md", ACCEPTED_STUB)
    199 +
    200 +    missing = missing_inputs(args.run_folder)
    201 +    if missing:
    202 +        print(f"Run folder is missing required input files: {', '.join(missing)}")
    203 +        return 1
    204 +
    205 +    prompt_path = args.run_folder / "MODEL_REQUEST.md"
    206 +    command = build_aider_command(args, prompt_path)
    207 +
    208 +    start = time.monotonic()

    210 +        command,
    211 +        cwd=PROJECT_ROOT,
    212 +        capture_output=True,

    215 +    )
    216 +    elapsed_seconds = time.monotonic() - start
    217 +
    218 +    (args.run_folder / "OUTPUT.md").write_text(build_output_text(result), encoding="utf-8")
    219 +    (args.run_folder / "METRICS.json").write_text(

    221 +        encoding="utf-8",
    222 +    )
    223 +
    224 +    validation = validate_run_folder(args.run_folder)
    225 +    if validation.valid:
    226 +        print(f"Validated run folder: {args.run_folder}")
    227 +    else:
    228 +        print(f"Invalid run folder: {args.run_folder}")
    229 +        if validation.path_error:
    230 +            print(f"Error: {validation.path_error}")
    231 +        if validation.missing_files:
    232 +            print("Missing required files:")
    233 +            for filename in validation.missing_files:
    234 +                print(f"- {filename}")
    235 +
    236 +    return 0 if result.returncode == 0 and validation.valid else 1
    237 +
    238 +
    239 +if __name__ == "__main__":
    240 +    raise SystemExit(main())

    349 +def parse_token_count(raw_value: str) -> int | None:
    350 +    value = raw_value.strip().lower()
    351 +    multiplier = 1
    352 +    if value.endswith("k"):
    353 +        multiplier = 1000
    354 +        value = value[:-1]
    355 +    try:
    356 +        return int(float(value) * multiplier)
    357 +    except ValueError:
    358 +        return None
    359 +
    360 +
    361 +def summarize_aider_output(output_text: str, selected_files: Sequence[str]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    362 +    applied_edits = re.findall(r"Applied edit to (.+)", output_text)
    363 +    token_match = re.search(r"Tokens:\s+([0-9.]+k?) sent,\s+([0-9.]+k?) received\.", output_text)
    364 +    tokens_sent = parse_token_count(token_match.group(1)) if token_match else None
    365 +    tokens_received = parse_token_count(token_match.group(2)) if token_match else None
    366 +    selected_set = set(selected_files)
    367 +    applied_set = set(applied_edits)
    368 +    return {
    369 +        "thinking_block_present": "► **THINKING**" in output_text,
    370 +        "answer_block_present": "► **ANSWER**" in output_text,
    371 +        "applied_edits": applied_edits,
    372 +        "applied_edit_count": len(applied_edits),
    373 +        "all_selected_files_edited": bool(selected_set) and selected_set.issubset(applied_set),
    374 +        "unexpected_edit_paths": sorted(applied_set - selected_set),
    375 +        "tokens_sent": tokens_sent,
    376 +        "tokens_received": tokens_received,
    377 +        "context_error_detected": "exceeds the available context size" in output_text,
    378 +        "timeout_hint_detected": "timed out" in output_text.lower() or "timeout" in output_text.lower(),
    379 +    }

    191 +def build_read_snippet_text(
    192 +    read_path: str,
    193 +    source_lines: Sequence[str],
    194 +    max_lines: int | None,
    195 +    max_chars: int | None,
    196 +) -> tuple[str, dict[str, Any]]:
    197 +    candidate_lines = list(source_lines if max_lines is None else source_lines[:max_lines])
    198 +    output_lines = [
    199 +        "# Read-only snippet",
    200 +        f"# Source: {read_path}",

    204 +
    205 +    for line in candidate_lines:

    207 +        if max_chars is not None and len(tentative) > max_chars:
    208 +            break
    209 +        output_lines.append(line)
    210 +        kept_count += 1
    211 +
    212 +    truncated = kept_count < len(source_lines)
    213 +    if truncated:
    214 +        trailer_lines = ["", f"[truncated after {kept_count} lines]"]
    215 +        tentative = "\n".join(output_lines + trailer_lines) + "\n"
    216 +        if max_chars is None or len(tentative) <= max_chars:
    217 +            output_lines.extend(trailer_lines)
    218 +
    219 +    snippet_text = "\n".join(output_lines) + "\n"
    220 +    metadata = {
    221 +        "source_line_count": len(source_lines),

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

• I pushed this to the real boundary. The current limit is no longer prompt packing; it is the Aider/LiteLLM transport itself on this endpoint as of June 7,
  2026.

  What I changed:

  - Hardened the manager wrapper in run_aider_worker.py with:
      - protocol-overhead budgeting
      - budget-aware read fitting
      - bundled read inputs
      - inline read digests
      - explicit fatal connection-error detection
      - richer metrics for retries and transport failures
  - Expanded coverage in test_run_aider_worker.py.
  - Updated operator docs in README.md.
  - Patched the local copied Aider code with opt-in hooks for skipping example chat and using a minimal prompt in base_coder.py.
  - Cleaned and reviewed the experiment trail in 2026-06-07_009_gemma-aider-read-overflow/REVIEW.md, 2026-06-07_017_gemma-aider-recheck-smoke/REVIEW.md, and
    2026-06-07_018_gemma-aider-actual-model-smoke/REVIEW.md.

  What I found:

  - Earlier validated Aider successes still stand for tiny tasks, including the three-file success in 2026-06-07_008_gemma-aider-three-file/REVIEW.md.
  - After that, every new Aider run degraded into the same retry loop, even after:
      - shrinking read context
      - bundling read files
      - inlining read digests
      - lowering usable budget
      - switching to the actual discovered model id
  - The decisive result is 2026-06-07_017_gemma-aider-recheck-smoke/METRICS.json: a one-file, no-read, ~70-token Aider smoke still failed with repeated
    OpenAIException - Connection error.
  - I separately verified that the bare local endpoint still works:
      - /v1/models still responds
      - a direct minimal chat call still returned ok
      - a direct system+user chat also worked
  - That means the current blocker is specifically the Aider/LiteLLM request path, not the Gemma server in general.

  Verification:

  - python3 -m unittest discover -s local_harness/tests
  - python3 -m unittest discover -s XX_backend/tests
  - git diff --check
  - python3 XX_backend/validate_agent_run.py 10_agent_runs/2026-06-07_009_gemma-aider-read-overflow
  - python3 XX_backend/validate_agent_run.py 10_agent_runs/2026-06-07_017_gemma-aider-recheck-smoke
  - python3 XX_backend/validate_agent_run.py 10_agent_runs/2026-06-07_018_gemma-aider-actual-model-smoke

  Current conclusion:

  - I did move the harness limit in a useful way: it now distinguishes context-shape failures from transport failures and records the latter cleanly.
  - I did not restore a successful Aider run after the transport went unhealthy.
  - The next real fix is lower-level than prompt packing.

  Next steps:

  1. Patch or replace the Aider/LiteLLM transport so it sends the same compact chat shape that the direct working endpoint accepts.
  2. Add a direct-edit fallback worker alongside Aider, so Gemma can still act as the grunt when Aider transport is down.
